# Firn library index (lib/)

GENERATED by `tools/gen_libs_index.py` -- do not edit by hand.

- **Before writing a new lib or function: read this file / `rg` in `lib/`. Do not rebuild what exists.**
- Regenerate: `bash tools/gen_libs_index.sh` (after every rebase onto main, never hand-merge this file).
- Gate: `tools/hygiene/check.sh` fails when this index no longer matches `lib/`.
- Purpose = first header comment of the file; missing = **NO DESCRIPTION** (list at the end).
- API = names in the module's `export { ... }` block (types, then functions, then constants; limited).
- Platforms = `name.<os>.fi` twins and `lib/@<os>/...` overlays. Tests = `tests/NNNN_*.fi` that `import` the module.
- Topic docs are only linked, not repeated here (see also docs/APPLICATION_LIBS.md, docs/MC-LIBS.md, docs/ASYNC.md, docs/COMPRESSION.md).

1577 modules in 82 lib directories. <!-- lib-fingerprint: a02a8004c425843d -->
